285:["$","ul","596",{"children":[["$","li","0",{"children":["Already absent is CONFIRMED: a delete answered 'not found' is classified ambiguous (errorCode already_absent), never a rejection, and the lookup that follows proves absence (evidence object_absent). CONFIRMED therefore means the object is absent, not that this action removed it; an object that never existed confirms the same way."]}],["$","li","1",{"children":["A lookup that still finds the object after a lost response proves SAFE_TO_RETRY: a repeated delete cannot duplicate an effect, and a retry that meets our own landed delete reads as already absent."]}],["$","li","2",{"children":["Irreversible: no compensator is declared and none can exist.
